shared_focus = {
	id = allies_joint_alliance
	
	dynamic = yes 
	
	icon = GFX_focus_ARG_join_the_allies
	

	x = 0
	y = 0

	#ENG
	offset = 
	{
		x = 68
		trigger = {has_focus_tree = british_focus}
	}
	#CAN
	offset = 
	{
		x = 64
		trigger = {has_focus_tree = canadian_focus}
	}
	#BRA
	offset = 
	{
		x = 80
		trigger = {has_focus_tree = brazil_tree}
	}
	#SAF
	offset = 
	{
		x = 58
		trigger = {has_focus_tree = south_african_focus}
	}

	#AST
	offset = 
	{
		x = 64
		trigger = {has_focus_tree = australian_focus}
	}
	#RAJ
	offset = 
	{
		x = 15
		y = 8
		trigger = {has_focus_tree = indian_focus_goe}
	}
	#FRA
	offset = 
	{
		x = 75
		trigger = {has_focus_tree = french_focus}
	}
	#FFRA
	offset = 
	{
		x = 60
		trigger = {has_focus_tree = free_french_focus}
	}

	
	allow_branch = {
		has_country_flag = shared_tree_clicked
	}

	cost = 0

	text_icon = NORDIC_focus_style

	available = {
		has_country_flag = accessible_shared_tree
		is_subject = no
	}
    bypass = 
    {
        is_in_faction_with = ENG
		is_ai = no
    }

	bypass_effect = {
		add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = allies_dynamic }
	}

	ai_will_do = {
		base = 1
		modifier = {
			factor = 100
			is_historical_focus_on = yes 
		}
	}

	available_if_capitulated = yes

	search_filters = { FOCUS_FILTER_POLITICAL }

	completion_reward = {
	    if = {
	        limit = { has_country_flag = accessible_shared_tree }

		
		    }
	}
}

joint_focus = {
	id = allies_political_cooperation
	icon = GFX_focus_generic_improve_the_administration
	prerequisite = { focus = allies_joint_alliance }

	x = 0
	y = 1
	relative_position_id = allies_joint_alliance
	cost = 5

	text_icon = NORDIC_focus_style

	available = {
		
			has_country_flag = accessible_shared_tree
}

	search_filters = { FOCUS_FILTER_POLITICAL }

	completion_reward_joint_originator = {
    every_country = {
        limit = {
            OR = {
                tag = ENG
				tag = CAN
                tag = SAF
				tag = BRA
				tag = FRA
                tag = AST
            }
        }
        add_political_power = 25
        add_stability = 0.1
        add_popularity = {
            ideology = democratic
            popularity = 0.1
        }
    }
}

}

joint_focus = {
	id = allies_lower_power_usage_policy
	icon = GFX_focus_ARG_develop_the_electricity_sector
	prerequisite = { focus = allies_political_cooperation }
	mutually_exclusive = {focus = allies_infra focus = allies_civ_eco_boost }

	x = 2
	y = 1
	relative_position_id = allies_political_cooperation
	cost = 5

	text_icon = NORDIC_focus_style

	available = {
		
			has_country_flag = accessible_shared_tree
}

	search_filters = { FOCUS_FILTER_POLITICAL }

	completion_reward = {
			if = {
				limit = { has_country_flag = accessible_shared_tree }
				custom_effect_tooltip = generic_skip_one_line_tt
				custom_effect_tooltip = allies_shared_tt
					add_to_variable = { 
						allies_energy_consumption = -0.15
						tooltip = factory_energy_consumption_tt
					}
				}
				custom_effect_tooltip = generic_skip_one_line_tt
				custom_effect_tooltip = faction_warning_tt
		}
}

joint_focus = {
	id = allies_blitzkrieg_phase1
	icon = GFX_focus_GER_prioritize_the_four_year_plan
	prerequisite = { focus = allies_blitzkrieg_economy }

	x = 0
	y = 1
	relative_position_id = allies_blitzkrieg_economy
	cost = 5

	text_icon = NORDIC_focus_style

	available = {
			has_country_flag = accessible_shared_tree
}

	bypass = 
	{
		has_global_flag = allies_financial_collapse
	}

	bypass_effect = 
	{

	    	add_to_variable = { 
	    		allies_growth_gain = 0.05
	    		
	    	}

			add_to_variable = { 
	    		allies_mill_speed = 0.05
	    		
	    	}
	}

	search_filters = { FOCUS_FILTER_POLITICAL }

	completion_reward = {

		ROOT = 
		{
			add_days_mission_timeout = { mission = blitzkrieg_timer days = -120 } 
		}
		if = {			
	    	limit = { has_country_flag = accessible_shared_tree }

			custom_effect_tooltip = generic_skip_one_line_tt
			custom_effect_tooltip = allies_shared_tt

	    	add_to_variable = { 
	    		allies_growth_gain = 0.05
	    		tooltip = production_factory_efficiency_gain_factor_tt 
	    	}

			add_to_variable = { 
	    		allies_mill_speed = 0.05
	    		tooltip = production_speed_arms_factory_factor_tt
	    	}
		}
	}
}

	    	}

	    	}

#### BRAZIL - NAVY ####

joint_focus = {
	id = allies_brazil_navy_docks
	icon = GFX_goal_generic_construct_naval_dockyard
	prerequisite = { focus = allies_brazil_specialization }

	x = -1
	y = 1
	relative_position_id = allies_brazil_specialization
	cost = 5

	text_icon = NORDIC_focus_style

	available = {
			has_country_flag = accessible_shared_tree
}

	allow_branch = 
	{
		BRA = {has_country_flag = navy_specialization_flag}
	ROOT = {has_country_flag = shared_tree_clicked}
	}

	mutually_exclusive = {focus = allies_brazil_navy_output}

	search_filters = { FOCUS_FILTER_POLITICAL }

	completion_reward_joint_originator = 
	{
	    if = {
	        limit = { has_country_flag = accessible_shared_tree }

				BRA = 
				{
						every_core_state = {
							limit = {
								is_fully_controlled_by = ROOT
								is_coastal = yes
							}
							random_select_amount = 2
							add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 2
							add_building_construction = {
								type = dockyard
								level = 2
								instant_build = yes
							}
						}
							
				}
		    }

	prerequisite = { focus = allies_south_african_specialization }

	x = -1
	y = 1
	relative_position_id = allies_south_african_specialization
	cost = 5

	text_icon = NORDIC_focus_style

	available = {
			has_country_flag = accessible_shared_tree
}

	allow_branch = 
	{
		SAF = {has_country_flag = navy_specialization_flag}
	ROOT = {has_country_flag = shared_tree_clicked}
	}

	mutually_exclusive = {focus = allies_south_africa_navy_output}

	search_filters = { FOCUS_FILTER_POLITICAL }

	completion_reward_joint_originator = 
	{
	    if = {
	        limit = { has_country_flag = accessible_shared_tree }

				SAF = 
				{
						every_core_state = {
							limit = {
								is_fully_controlled_by = ROOT
								is_coastal = yes
							}
							random_select_amount = 2
							add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 2
							add_building_construction = {
								type = dockyard
								level = 2
								instant_build = yes
							}
						}
							
				}
		    }
	}
}

					name = allies_radio_bonus

				}
		    }
	}
